# Wrath build-12340 opcode coverage

## Scope and status contract

Static dispatch audit at client **282cb1ea47862380b3f3328f3cc978c5bb628860** (arena-team integration), carried forward to integrated **8b261963** after reviewing its changes: guild-bank log/text/query/swap/tab codecs/exports and Material test/docs only, no new reachable dispatch/writer path. Guild-bank log/text codecs remain missing transport, not handled. No client launch or live packet capture was performed. Source evidence is not a successful gameplay-session claim.

The universe is the local AzerothCore `src/server/game/Server/Protocol/Opcodes.h` enum, cross-checked with every registration in `Opcodes.cpp`, plus constructor/send references under `src/server`. All **1,312 distinct entries** are listed, including legacy/unused registry entries rather than pretending each can actually be emitted. There are 618 CMSG, 583 SMSG, 105 bidirectional MSG, four internal UMSG, and two TC9 redirect names. `NUM_MSG_TYPES` is a sentinel, not a packet. Realm/SRP authentication uses a separate byte-opcode namespace described below.

- **handled**: a reachable native decoder and semantic/protocol consumer, or an admitted native encoder/writer path. This is wire coverage, **not full feature parity**. Outbound transport-only, gated producers and incomplete feedback are explicitly qualified.
- **capture-only**: decoded packets/fields terminate in an ordered FIFO or retained native state, without a corresponding semantic/UI feed. Retained-map snapshots are not lossless packet FIFOs.
- **missing**: no reachable path, intentional rejection, compatibility-only observation, or materially partial handling. For partial rows the working subset is documented; do not discard it or infer new movement authority. A codec alone is not support.
- Bidirectional rows list **S→C then C→S** statuses independently. Internal UMSG entries are marked missing solely to keep the enum exhaustive; they are not actionable external gaps.
- **core**: login/session, ordinary movement/combat, quests, basic communication and essential item interactions; **common**: frequently used social/services/group features; **niche**: optional/specialized flows; **GM-debug**: admin, cheats, debug and obsolete development operations. Priority is player impact, independent of implementation status. Registry-only legacy entries are not automatically actionable.

### Evidence and limitations

Client paths below are repository-relative. `A/`, `P/`, `U/` in outbound evidence mean `crates/wrathplus-app/src/`, `crates/wrathplus-protocol/src/`, and `crates/wrathplus-ui/src/`. AC paths are relative to its `src/server/` tree (reference-only checkout `../azerothcore-playerbots/core`). Line references describe this baseline and may drift.

AC evidence labels distinguish **proven_send_site** (opcode construction plus a locally traced send), **proven_constructor_only** (packet construction/initialization, send may be through another helper), **not_proven_reference_only** (dynamic dispatch/table/mention, not proof of emission), and **registry_only_no_source_refs**. These are conservative static evidence classes, not claims that a constructor-only packet cannot be sent. Dynamic serializers and movement tables can share send helpers. `STATUS_NEVER` on SMSG is the normal server-opcode registration status, **not evidence of obsolescence**. Client Handle_NULL does not establish a working server feature.

Malformed known packets often become `Unhandled`/`PacketDropped` rather than fatal errors; a row means only the supported valid payload subset. There is no unrestricted fallback to the Vanilla decoder. Generic framing/recording is not semantic support.

### Bounded core refresh at `395b1732`

Pet-name (`0x0052/0053`) and WHO/WHOIS (`0x0062..0065`) rows below were re-audited through transport and visible consumers on this branch. Other rows retain the original audit baseline above; this is not a fresh whole-client census. Manager accepted the documented default-3 WHO routing and BG/arena augmentation limitations. Final gates: protocol **548 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,628 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. Codec, FIFO ownership/privacy, per-receipt feed and real-handler reset regressions are in `wrath_who_tests`, `net::wrath_who_capture` tests, `ui_social::feed::native_tests`, `names_native_pet_tests` and `net/io/wrath/{pet_name,who}_tests`. No live client was launched.

Environmental/death durability (`0x01FC/02BD`) rows were separately re-audited in `fb261e3f`: native codecs, dispatch and existing semantic feedback are active. Registered-handler regressions verify single feedback per sink and unchanged native item durability. Post-`9e59bad2` gates: App **4,634 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**; Protocol **553 passed, 0 failed**; UI **1,825 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored**. This bounded update does not re-audit other rows.

Spell failures (`0x0133/0138`) now have strict native dispatch and one cross-opcode receipt FIFO, retaining duplicates and absent versus present argument words. FIFO retention is session-unbounded until Wrath Connected/disconnect clear; no silent drop policy is implied. The strict 0/4/8-byte pet-tail policy rejects partial/excess words for lossless framing, not as a claim of native rejection. Only seven proven pet keys are presented by key, not native numeric message ID; no generic fallback or duplicate interruption cleanup is introduced. Native evidence: build-12340 handlers `00809c70`, `00806c30`, `00805610`, table `00AC8AF0`; unresolved context-list/global/event semantics remain unsupported. Both rows conservatively count as capture-only despite the documented pet presentation subset.

Spell/aura logs (`0x0150/0151/024B/024C/024E/024F/0250/0260/0262/0263/027B/032F/0333` and the registry-only `014F/01D1/01D6/027C/03AA/0404`) were re-audited against AzerothCore in this unit; see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-SPELL-LOGS`. Strict native codecs in `wrath_logs`, existing combat-log owners, 0333 capture-only; registry-only rows stay deliberately undecoded. Gates: Protocol **583 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,658 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Spell control, pet and visual feedback (`0x0135/0139/013A/015B/0173/0178/01DE/01F3/01F7/0266/0267/02C6/0324/0325/039D/0413` inbound; `0x013B/015C/0174..0177/026B/026D/0279/029B/02EA/02F3/0375/03F8/0414/047A` outbound; 13 registry-only rows) were re-audited against AzerothCore in this unit; see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-SPELL-CONTROL`. Combo points now come from the native packet (no 3.3.5 descriptor carries them). Spell modifiers stay partial (96-bit family mask vs a 64-row table). Gates: Protocol **591 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,666 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M1, self authority orders (`0x00C7/00DE/00DF/00E2/00E4/00E6/00EF/00F2..00F5/02DA/02DC/02DE/0343/0381/0383/045C/04CE/04D0/0516` and their ACKs) plus the observer `0x00C5`: see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-MOVEMENT-ORDERS`. These orders were fatal in the Wrath reader; they now go through the MovementAuthority FIFO with native ACKs. Gates: Protocol **607 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,681 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M2, swimming and observer speed echoes: see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-MOVEMENT-ORDERS`. Swimming reaches the wire (SWIMMING with its pitch) instead of holding the player; swimmers' relays decode; `MSG_MOVE_SET_*_SPEED` and `SMSG_SPLINE_SET_*_SPEED` for the 1.12 kinds become `SpeedChanged`. Gates: Protocol **619 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,690 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M3, observers (flight and transport relays, mode/knockback/collision/time-skip echoes), `SMSG_MULTIPLE_MOVES` split through the authority path, mount special anim, `CMSG_MOVE_TIME_SKIPPED`, dismount capture: see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-MOVEMENT-ORDERS`. Gates: Protocol **631 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,704 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M5a, flight: CAN_FLY/FLYING controller mode with the ascend/descend keys, `CMSG_MOVE_SET_FLY` and the vertical edges, flight speeds into the controller, and the flight and pitch-rate observer echoes; see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-FLIGHT`. Gates: Protocol **644 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,743 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**; UI **1,881 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M5b, control hand-over, vehicles, root and pitch keys: the active mover follows `SMSG_CLIENT_CONTROL_UPDATE`, a vehicle or possessed unit is driven and acked from its own pose, roots reach the controller and the wire reports ROOT, `NOT_ACTIVE_MOVER`/dismiss/change-seats writers, the pitch keys, and the vehicle UI feed (`net/wrath_vehicle.rs`); see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-CONTROL`. Gates: Protocol **654 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,782 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**; UI **1,912 passed, 0 failed, 1 ignored**. No live client was launched.

Movement M6, polish: swim ascend/descend, take-off from the water, relayed fliers (Wrath FLYING onto the shared FLYING bit, pitched dead reckoning, Fly 135), and a second Ghidra round narrowing the four labelled C→S rows (none proven far enough to emit); see `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-FLIGHT` and `https://github.com/artivus-labs/wrathplus/wiki/WRATH-CONTROL`. No row changes status. Gates: Protocol **654 passed, 0 failed**; App **4,785 passed, 0 failed, 12 ignored**. No live client was launched.

## Remaining work

Every row still **missing** above, counted per direction like the totals (495 = 189 S→C + 306 C→S), falls in exactly one bucket. Classified at this revision from the table plus AzerothCore `Opcodes.cpp` registrations and send sites (`7f12e89e`). Reproduce: a C→S row is out of scope when AC registers it `STATUS_NEVER`, `Handle_NULL` or as a server opcode; an S→C row is out of scope when AC has no constructor or send (registry only, or only a commented-out reference).

## Ordered core/common work queue

1. **Ordinary communication/social:** native MESSAGECHAT, channel notifications beyond joined/left, send/join/leave and friend/contact queries/results. Existing channel roster support is not general chat support. Coordinate App-owned visible feeds and drains.
2. **Essential inventory/item use:** USE_ITEM, equip/swap/split/destroy/open and their replies. Verify native target layouts, bag/slot authority and shared ownership before enabling commands; never route a Vanilla body by opcode coincidence.
3. **World transfer and movement safety:** NEW_WORLD lacks admitted WorldportAck; teleport, speed orders/ACKs, swimming/flight/transport and MULTIPLE_MOVES remain unsupported or fenced. This is a separate movement-authority assignment, not permission to weaken guards. Preserve stale/foreign/epoch rejection.
4. **Login metadata:** tutorial flags, account-data-times and addon-info are missing in live dispatch. Connected currently synthesizes an empty addon list and no tutorial flags. Compatibility bootstrap observation is not a feed.
5. **Common interaction gaps:** trade/bank/auction, mail mutations, remaining party controls; review existing codecs for unreachable transport before creating duplicate models. Mail query and GuildQuery writers exist but production supports checks gate them.
6. **Pet/talent/achievement and captured families:** pet bar has a real consumer but command coverage is incomplete. Talents/criteria/loot-owner state is retained without corresponding feeds. Guild, arena, stable, LFG, guild-bank read replies and saved-instance receipts are intentionally capture-only; App owns future reducers/feeds/drains.

Each implementation is a bounded unit with codec/adapter tests and full protocol/App gates, current manager baseline merged before report, and explicit sole consumer ownership. This document is a planning census, not authorization for cross-owner movement/UI changes.

## Bootstrap and producer qualifications



- A/net/io.rs:197–222 selects `wrath::spawn_threads` for Wrath12340, not the Vanilla writer loop.
- A/net/io/wrath.rs:181–202 calls native `wrath_auth::logon`; 264–298 connects world and enumerates characters; 312–347 selects character and begins player login; 347–378 splits/install writer and publishes epoch-bound command endpoint.
- **Login-server commands are a different namespace**, NOT CMSG world opcodes: `CMD_AUTH_LOGON_CHALLENGE 0x00`, `CMD_AUTH_LOGON_PROOF 0x01`, `CMD_REALM_LIST 0x10`. Constants P/wrath_auth.rs:15–22; actual writes 131–149,219–228,243–244; orchestration/server-proof verification 376–419. Version 3.3.5, build 12340, challenge protocol 8. Realm refresh is also real (A/net/io/wrath.rs:223–225,317–320).
- P/wrath_world.rs:305–324 sends AUTH_SESSION with initially unencrypted header, then initializes header crypto. Proof/body: 793–841; includes realm ID and the compressed stock addon block (12340's writer `0x5f5440`: count, the 23 `## Secure: 1` built-ins with `.pub` flag/modulus CRC and `.url` CRC, banned-cache trailer 0; P/types/addons.rs). Character enum actual send 375–397; player-login GUID actual send 444–451.
- P/wrath_world.rs:522–539 transfers original write socket/encrypter through split. `WrathWorldWriter::send_raw` (610–618) invokes framing at 844–864: BE u16 size including four opcode bytes, LE u32 opcode, header crypto when enabled, then body. Failed writes poison the writer (867–887).
- All installed-session app/control packets share A/net/io/wrath.rs writer loop: internal replies 1120–1125, periodic ping 1189–1201, commands 1224–1332. Ping interval is 30 seconds (A/net/io.rs:86), armed only after player-login writer install, not while parked on character selection.
- Login-server PIN/matrix/token/unknown security flags are rejected (P/wrath_auth.rs:175–183). Warden is unsupported/fatal (P/wrath_world.rs:363,629–634; A/net/io/wrath.rs:565). No Warden reply claimed.

## Authority and writer restrictions (apply in addition to per-table status)

1. App commands require a live installed writer and exact immutable session epoch (A/net/io/wrath.rs:1224–1235,1629–1631). SessionCommandSender stamping and Connected endpoint replacement: A/net.rs:508–558. Old internal controls ignored at 1121–1122,1159. Reader exit revokes under the movement mutex before synchronous retirement (599–621).
2. Movement and SET_ACTIVE_MOVER require matching epoch/self GUID, observed self-create, self-control, no pending authority order and not rooted (770–783,809–828). Initial own CREATE_OBJECT self flag supplies control unless explicitly decided earlier (693–722). Foreign mover/control transfer is fatal (746–758,1237–1245), not possession/vehicle support.
3. Selection/melee/posture/sheath and solo loot use `with_player_action`: matching self/epoch, observed self-create and self-control; root does **not** block these (786–798,1372–1377,1598–1607). Melee denial silently drops; loot denial returns error and leads to fatal retirement. Loot GUID must be nonzero, slot <18; sheath 0..2, supported stand states 0/1/3/8 (1338–1395; P/wrath_combat.rs encoder validation).
4. Ordinary movement is only validated **on-foot** including jump/fall/land: no pitch, transport, swimming, flight or arbitrary flags (A/net/io/wrath.rs:1633–1679; P/wrath_ground.rs:13–46,198–320). Secondary flags are zero. Finite pose/tail, flag consistency, opcode/flag agreement required. Shared MoveKind containing swim opcodes does NOT enable them.
5. Root/unroot and unset-can-fly ACKs are genuine server-triggered flows (A/net/io/wrath.rs:444–477,874–963,1682–1740): matching self and FIFO order/counter, stored own on-foot pose, pending barriers. Up to eight pre-self orders, two-second timeout; duplicate/stale/foreign orders fail closed. Root can emit STOP before ACK. `CMSG_MOVE_SET_CAN_FLY_ACK` is used for **unset** can-fly only: not support for granting flight.
6. Taxi spline completion has independent one-use authority: own current-epoch self-created matching unconsumed flying/noncyclic/nontransport spline; command flags must be zero (A/net/io/wrath/taxi_authority.rs:9–70). Not general spline/vehicle control.
7. **Do not apply movement guards to every opcode.** NativeSession, item query, guild, LFG and arena routes generally write after live-writer/epoch checks without `with_player_action` (1265–1308). Server permissions still apply; transport does not infer interaction/leadership/proposal authority. LFG proposal lifecycle guard is explicitly TODO (1398–1403). Loot-roll vote >3 is dropped (998); native encoder accepts 0..3 (1558–1566).
8. `NetCommands::supports` (A/net.rs:578–673) is **narrower than route**. Some producers consult it, others send directly. Its omission is not a universal writer prohibition, but it disables the production guild-identity and mail paths described below.

## Producer evidence by family

- **Bootstrap/lifecycle/control:** credentials A/login/mod.rs:161; selection A/char_select/mod.rs:310; active mover A/player/wire_in.rs:112–138; Logout/ForceLogout A/ui_logout.rs:144,178 (both encode LOGOUT_REQUEST). Ground movement A/player/movement_net.rs:268,474. Taxi completion A/player/server_ride.rs:203–224. Time-sync response P/wrath_events.rs:510–524 -> A/net/io/wrath.rs:544–550 ->1123–1124; exactly four-byte server counter required.
- **Object queries:** A/names.rs:173–186,214–230; A/items.rs:412–430; A/go_templates.rs:101–117. Object-create invokes caches at A/net/objects.rs:532–542. These are actual ask-once/cache-miss producers.
- **Melee/loot:** selection A/target/mod.rs:85; posture A/player/posture.rs:87; targeting/casting melee paths and sheath/posture feed. Native solo loot A/ui_loot/wrath.rs:141–171; money/item/release A/ui_loot/mod.rs:741–817,940–997. Limited native loot handling is not all loot types/master loot.
- **Spells/action bar:** A/spell/cast_send.rs:138–180,702–712 constructs/sends None/unit/destination/source/gameobject/item targets; A/spell/targeting/world.rs:32–66 commits world targeting. CancelCast A/spell/inflight.rs:327–345; CancelAura A/ui_items/mod.rs:758–763 and A/ui_spellbook.rs:456–479; SetActionButton A/ui_action/drain.rs:402 and ranks.rs:51. Six cast commands collapse to ONE CMSG_CAST_SPELL. Corpse-target cast and USE_ITEM are NOT covered.
- **NPC/quest:** click services A/target/click.rs:1466–1503; gossip selection A/ui_gossip/mod.rs:350–381; text query A/ui_gossip/net.rs:109; quest status/taxi status A/quest_markers/query.rs:175–203; accept/complete/reward A/ui_quest/mod.rs:668–742; quest query/share/abandon A/ui_quest_log.rs:66,900,916; share confirmation A/ui_quest_share.rs:238. Quest push-result command is NOT routed.
- **Vendor/trainer/taxi/binder:** vendor purchase/sell/buyback/repair A/ui_merchant/mod.rs:709–797 and A/ui_items/drain.rs:510–533; trainer purchase A/ui_trainer/mod.rs:531 and NPC service dispatch A/target/click.rs:619–622,1480–1503; taxi A/ui_taxi/mod.rs:263–273; binder native interaction is guarded by its App owner, not a blanket writer authority check.
- **Party/raid:** Lua U/script/party.rs:420–647 and U/script/wrath_party_assignment.rs:133–171 -> direct sends A/ui_party/feed.rs:935–1131. Roster-driven stats request A/ui_party/net.rs:68–79,419–446. Leader/uninvite producers exist but those commands are not natively routed.
- **Guild:** U/script/guild.rs:471–539 -> A/ui_guild/feed.rs:295–339 sends info/roster/invite/accept/decline/promote/demote/remove/MOTD (roster throttled 10 seconds). **GuildQuery is GATED:** A/ui_guild/mod.rs:183–193 calls supports, which omits it. Route exists but this identity path never emits in Wrath. Other guild verbs not in native route are dropped.
- **Channels:** U/script/wrath_channel_bindings.rs:147–155 -> wrath_channel_roster.rs:43–60 queues Watch then DisplayList -> **Wrath-only direct sends** A/ui_chat/native_feed.rs:92–113. This is real active output, unlike chat send/join/leave.
- **Mail: GATED**, not active: A/ui_mail/mod.rs:193–219 uses send_supported; CheckInbox/refresh at 673–692 and A/ui_mail/net.rs:225–239 does likewise. supports omits both native mail queries. Native receive packet ownership does not activate these senders.
- **Stable:** list NPC interaction A/target/click.rs:1502–1503 ->619–622 is active. Mutations have Lua intents U/script/stable.rs:174–182,375–435 -> A/ui_stable/mod.rs:258–280, but require StableOpen. Native replies only enter packet FIFO with no UI drain (A/ui_stable/wrath.rs:1–23); legacy StableOpen feed A/ui_stable/net.rs:12–36 consumes a different event. Mark mutations PARTIAL, not established end-to-end Wrath stable UI.
- **LFG, arena, guild-bank queries: TRANSPORT only.** No production construction/send found for LfgCommand variants, lock-info requests, ArenaTeamCommand variants, GuildBankQuery variants. App declarations explicitly document this (A/net.rs:1903–1907,2087–2090). Native guild-bank owner is a receive FIFO, not a query initiator (A/ui_guild_bank.rs:1–25). No claimed UI lifecycle/authority.


## Audit reproducibility

The table is the numeric-order union of the AC enum (excluding its sentinel), exact registrations, source constructor/send-reference audit, explicit reachable inbound dispatch graph and native outbound writer whitelist. Every enum entry appears exactly once; MSG has independent directional statuses. Inbound partial/unsupported/observation-only entries are deliberately classified missing rather than inflated into handled. AC evidence is attached to each row, and outbound family producer caveats above remain part of the status. No server source or assets are included. Re-audit at each integration baseline; adding a module/constant alone must not change status.
